## Architecture and demonstrated capability

- **Verified now in source:** `C:\AI-BS\backend\core\coding_chat.py`: `request_prompt`, `is_coding_request`, `coding_request`, `_generate`. It recognizes bounded English fix/edit/modify/change/implement/refactor requests that include verification, refuses execution in plan/review mode, admits one request per backend process through `_ACTIVE`, requires one backticked Git repository, 1-5 existing Python implementation files and 1-5 operator-selected `python -m unittest ...` checks. It rejects edits to named test modules and model output outside the allowed files. `_generate` calls local Ollama `stehouwer_llm:latest` on `127.0.0.1:11434` with bounded context/response and no proxy inheritance. These limits are application controls, not an OS sandbox.
- **Verified now in source:** `C:\AI-BS\backend\core\coding_workflow.py`: `_relative`, `_inside`, `_save`, `get_coding_task`, `run_coding_task`. The runner validates Python source with `C:\AI-BS\backend\core\code_quality_gate.py`, creates a retained detached Git worktree under `C:\AI-BS\saved_data\coding_tasks\<task-id>`, saves requested original bytes/hashes, writes proposals in the worktree, runs explicit Python argv checks as host processes with reduced environment and 1-120-second per-check timeout, saves real return codes/stdout/stderr and a diff, and marks success only when checks pass, the diff is nonempty, and requested originals remain unchanged. It does **not** apply to the original checkout, repair failures, recover interrupted execution, provide OS isolation, or release artifacts.
- **Verified now in source:** active chat handlers in `C:\AI-BS\backend\core\hybrid_reasoning_engine.py` (`chat_hybrid_endpoint`, `/api/v1/hybrid-chat/stream`) and `C:\AI-BS\backend\routers\chat_router.py` (`generate_chat_completion`, `/api/chat`) call the adapter. `C:\AI-BS\backend\AI_BS_Backend.py` contains compatibility chat handlers and `AdminExecutionBoundaryMiddleware`; registration order matters because the earlier router handlers can shadow later compatibility routes. `C:\AI-BS\backend\tools\tool_registry.py` exposes `run_coding_task` and `get_coding_task`. The main FastAPI app protects chat, executive, mission, and tool routes with its admin boundary; new review/apply endpoints must be protected at the backend as well.
- **Prior verification, retained evidence:** 19 focused adapter checks, 11 live FastAPI/Go chat checks, and 16 controlled restart/auth/health checks passed for v5.329.11. The live report records anonymous 403 on normal and hybrid chat through ports 8080 and 8000, plan mode without execution, a readable successful coding stream, a real unittest exit 0, preserved originals, and an ordinary greeting response. Example task `de46cd20c6214fbb949a03183e2a24db` is a **legacy unsealed task** and cannot safely be applied by Phase 1. Reports: `C:\Users\footb\Documents\Codex\2026-10-22\c-ai-bs\outputs\AI-BS_Coding_Chat_5_329_11_Verification_2026-10-23.json`, `AI-BS_Coding_Chat_5_329_11_Live_2026-10-23.json`, `AI-BS_Runtime_5_329_11_Live_Verification_2026-10-23.json`; narrative `C:\AI-BS\saved_data\project_reports\2026-10-23\AI-BS_Stehouwer_Coding_Chat_5_329_11.md`. ## Diagnostics, causes, and approaches to avoid

- **Verified now:** `coding_workflow.py` persists only requested-file input hashes and a mutable worktree/diff. It does not save repository path, a sealed proposal digest, immutable tested source, or all verification-input hashes. An apply implementation must add these before considering any newly produced task applicable; old task records remain review-only.
- **Prior finding:** earlier compatibility handler edits alone did not affect live chat because route order registered other handlers first. Update the active router paths and verify through HTTP, not by source inspection alone.
- **Prior finding:** an `Unauthorized` plain-text response was parsed as JSON in the UI. That does not establish a RapidAPI subscription/key issue. Do not “fix” by clearing browser permissions, killing all Chrome, or bypassing auth. Upload's empty/non-JSON response error is separate.
- **Prior finding:** model visibility/404 problems followed an Ollama update and changed registry visibility. Preserve `C:\AI-BS\Modelfile*` and customized model files; check `/api/tags` and actual model registry before rebuilding or pulling weights. Do not assume GPU driver is the cause of JavaScript temporal-dead-zone errors such as `Cannot access 'd' before initialization`.
- **Prior finding:** an Ollama restart script rejected ambiguous port 11434 owners; `Get-NetTCPConnection` does not support `-AddressFamily`. Do not pick the first PID or kill by port alone. Check listening state, executable path/command line and loaded models before any restart.
- **Verified source limitation:** Git worktree separation is not VM isolation. Python AST checks catch some defects; they do not make generated code safe. Generic `trainer.py` is not a proven self-maintenance engine: `game_trainer/core/trainer.py` is game oriented, while `backend/bullshit_trainer.py` can start a background loop at import. Do not import/activate it casually.
- Do not use whole-repo reset, mass mirror sync, broad service restarts, automatic model rebuilding, or automatic publishing while this checkout contains unrelated user work.

## Security and operating constraints

- Admin-only backend enforcement for code generation, review, application, cancellation and recovery. Authentication must be verified at the actual HTTP routes, including through the Go gateway. Never treat a UI button as authorization. A model proposal cannot approve its own application.
- One user task at a time, low model usage, one automatic repair maximum. Preserve originals, proposed files, failed attempts, logs, backups, and all creative documents in labeled folders. Do not forward personal text, especially to Dennis; outbound email/SMS/Discord remains inactive without explicit activation. Keep local secrets out of frontend, prompts, reports, and this handoff. A previously pasted RapidAPI credential must not be copied into source or reproduced here.
- Routine local edits, verification, and scoped AI-BS restarts are preapproved. Ask before deletion, DNS changes, purchases, disruptive host changes, or expanding permissions. Publishing only after verified release gates; native signing prerequisites must be checked. Do not assert every claimed platform capability is verified.
- Phase 4 expanded execution must fail closed if disposable Windows VM isolation is unavailable: guest network off by default, only approved inputs/outputs, no host documents/secrets/model originals, process-tree cancellation, time/output bounds. Existing small host workflow remains explicitly trusted local execution until migrated.
- Performance evidence: distinguish inference, tool, application and first response timing; record hardware/model/workload/cold-warm/load; use at least 10 comparable runs per reported condition with median, p95 and failure count. Offline behavior must be demonstrated without disrupting the user's host network.

**Verified October 23, 2026:** Local source is **v5.329.14**. The new `backend/core/coding_recovery.py` persists coding-request admissions before model generation under `C:\AI-BS\saved_data\coding_requests`, identifies a worker by PID and creation time, lists recent task/request states, and performs read-only reconciliation. `backend/core/coding_chat.py` updates admissions with attempt IDs and terminal state. `backend/core/coding_workflow.py` records worker identity. Its three changed component files have four matching mirrors. No recovery action reruns inference, tests, or application.

- A live worker still running retains its state. Once its process has ended, a nonterminal generation or test task is marked `INTERRUPTED` and requires an explicit new run. Completed results are returned without mutation. For interrupted application, recovery compares backup, original and proposed hashes: all exact proposed bytes may be marked `APPLIED`; all original bytes are marked `INTERRUPTED`; mixed or unknown bytes remain `PARTIAL` or `CONFLICT` for review. Recovery writes only task metadata, not project files.

## Phase 4 read-only feasibility and stop boundary

**Verified October 23, 2026:** WMI reports **Microsoft Windows 11 Home**, build 26300, 64-bit; registry `EditionID=Core` corroborates the Home edition (its legacy `ProductName` text says Windows 10 Home and should not override WMI's Windows 11 identification). Firmware virtualization reports enabled. `VirtualMachinePlatform` is enabled, and WSL2 Ubuntu distributions exist, but WSL2 is not the planned disposable **Windows** VM. No `Get-VM`, `New-VM`, `Start-VM`, Docker, VirtualBox, VMware, WindowsSandbox.exe, or vmconnect.exe was found in the checked commands and standard installation paths. This is a targeted inventory, not a proof that no VM software exists anywhere on any drive. Free space at inspection: C: 198.8 GB; D: 56.5 GB; E: 107.5 GB; F: 61.4 GB.

Microsoft states that the Hyper-V role cannot be installed on Windows 11 Home and Windows Sandbox is unsupported on Home: [Hyper-V installation](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows-server/virtualization/hyper-v/get-started/install-hyper-v?pivots=windows-server), [Windows Sandbox editions](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows/security/application-security/application-isolation/windows-sandbox/). Oracle documents VirtualBox as a Windows 11 host option and Windows 11 guests, so it is a candidate pending driver/host compatibility and guest licensing review: [VirtualBox introduction](https://docs.oracle.com/en/virtualization/virtualbox/7.1/user/Introduction.html). A Windows 11 guest needs at least 64 GB of storage and other VM requirements per [Microsoft](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows/whats-new/windows-11-requirements).

**Result:** Phase 4 isolation acceptance has **not passed**. No hypervisor, guest image, host feature, network setting, or license was changed. Larger/unattended execution remains disabled under the approved plan, and phases 5–6 cannot be accepted as complete. The current small Python workflow remains trusted host execution with review gates. Next decision is a Windows VM route: provide an existing licensed guest image with a compatible Windows Home hypervisor (for example VirtualBox), or authorize an edition upgrade/other host arrangement. Installation and any purchase/reboot require explicit user review because these are disruptive host changes under the user's standing rule. When selected, first validate a disposable guest with no network, no host document mounts, limited task exchange, cancellation, time/output bounds and host access denial; fail closed if unavailable. Do not treat a `sandbox` folder or WSL2 alone as satisfying this gate.

## Continuation checkpoint: v5.329.15

Verified October 23, 2026. The prior goal turn made progress by creating the handoff and implementing local review, repair, and recovery; this continuation made further source changes and gathered stronger live evidence. The full six-phase goal remains **active and incomplete**. No Windows VM was installed, no host edition/network setting changed, and no Firebase/native artifact was published.

### Correctness repairs before expanding execution

- `backend/core/coding_locks.py` is new: `record_lock`, `task_lock`, `repository_lock` use process-owned OS file locks. Review/apply/recovery serialize state changes, and different tasks cannot apply to the same repository concurrently. The OS releases these locks if a worker dies; a persistent `state.lock` or repository lock file alone is not evidence of a running operation. Legacy `apply.lock` plus an incomplete journal stays conservative until recovery inspection.
- `backend/core/coding_review.py`: `_current` now validates frozen original copies as well as source/proposal/test files and real check evidence; `_review_fingerprint` binds approval to the exact proposal, file list and check evidence. The token and fingerprint are rechecked after locking. Prior code allowed separate task applications to race and did not validate retained original copies or failed check evidence. New regression fixtures reproduced those defects before the fixes. Seals now use **schema version 2**, with `checks_hash`; old schema-1 proposals need fresh verification before application. Applied historical results remain inspectable.
- `backend/core/coding_workflow.py`: proposal files cannot replace the designated unittest modules, even through direct core/tool calls. The unittest inputs must match between selected checkout and worktree; dirty verification files are rejected before running tests. Prior enforcement existed only in the chat adapter, so direct calls could bypass that safeguard.
- `backend/core/coding_recovery.py`: application recovery checks the journal's **entire** file set against the sealed proposal, ensures the backup is in the task's backup store, and checks repository identity. It refuses to label a subset of an application complete. Unknown/mixed bytes remain blocked; recovery writes metadata only.
- `coding_recovery.admit_request(..., source_inputs=...)` now retains exact model-input source/test bytes and their hashes under `saved_data/coding_requests/<request-id>/inputs`. `assert_request_inputs` runs after initial inference and before/after repair inference. If the checkout changed while the model was thinking, generation stops without launching the code task. The newer edit stays intact. This closes a gap where original hashes were previously first captured only after inference.

### Phase 4 controls implemented without changing Windows

- New `backend/core/coding_process.py`, `run_bounded_check`: launches a check suspended, assigns it to a Windows Job Object before resuming, captures bounded output, polls cancellation/deadline, records the real return code and elapsed time, and closes the job to terminate ordinary descendants. The job also closes on the worker's process exit. `pywin32` and `psutil` were already installed and pinned in `C:\AI-BS\requirements.txt`; no dependency or driver was installed. Failure to establish process control yields a failed check; no alternate unrestricted check runner is selected.
- `run_coding_task` defaults: per-check deadline 30 seconds (maximum 120); overall workflow budget 180 seconds (maximum 600); combined stdout/stderr capture 262,144 bytes per check (maximum 1,048,576). These limits are recorded in task evidence. Limit errors and cancellation cannot produce a verified seal and do not trigger model repair. `execution_mode` is explicitly `trusted_host`.
- `request_cancellation(task_id)` and authenticated `POST /api/chat/coding-tasks/{task_id}/cancel` queue cancellation for worktree/check phases. Saved coding tasks shows **Cancel checks**, Refresh, review and recovery. Cancellation of an ongoing Ollama generation request is **not yet implemented**; do not claim full task cancellation across inference. The UI explicitly labels checks as running on the Windows host while VM isolation is pending.

### Current inference and cancellation architecture

- `C:\AI-BS\backend\core\coding_chat.py`: `_generate` now orchestrates an admitted inference worker. The previous direct local HTTP implementation is `_http_generate`, used inside that worker. `_GENERATION` holds the current admission per thread; `GenerationCancelled` and `_check_cancellation` stop initial generation or repair without falling through to code execution. Ordinary noncoding chat is unchanged. Existing local model name remains `stehouwer_llm:latest` and endpoint remains `http://127.0.0.1:11434/api/generate`; no model rebuild, pull, unload or configuration change occurred.
- New `C:\AI-BS\backend\core\coding_inference_worker.py`, `main`: accepts a bounded `input.json` inside the admitted request store, loads only the pure coding modules, performs one local inference request and emits JSON. It does not import the production FastAPI application or load its daemons. The parent launches it through the existing suspended-process/Windows-Job runner in `coding_process.run_bounded_check`, with an environment excluding backend/admin/API credentials. Inputs and actual worker stdout/stderr, exit status and elapsed time are retained under `saved_data/coding_requests/<request-id>/generation_<n>/input.json` and `result.json`.
- An inference call has a maximum 180-second worker deadline and 524,288-byte combined output cap. `coding_recovery.admit_request` persists a **300-second budget after admission**. Initial generation, optional repair and linked checks consume its remainder. The initial checkout/verification-input validation occurs before admission and retains its existing bounded Git-call timeouts; do not claim the 300 seconds covers that preceding validation.
- `coding_workflow.run_coding_task(..., on_created=...)` invokes an internal callback after its task record exists, before worktree creation. `coding_chat` uses it to link each attempt to the request immediately (`attempt_ids`, `current_task_id`, state `RUNNING`). This removes the earlier gap where a task killed during checking could not yet be linked to its admission. The callback is not exposed as model-supplied tool input.
- `coding_recovery.request_coding_cancellation(identifier)` accepts an existing task or request ID. For a request, it atomically records cancellation and forwards it to any linked current check task. `is_request_cancelled` is checked during bounded inference, between stages and before finalization. Final request state and incoming cancellation are serialized by the request OS lock, preventing a cancellation from being accepted after terminal completion. Unknown IDs fail without creating a spurious request folder.
- Authenticated `POST /api/chat/coding-tasks/{identifier}/cancel` now uses that unified helper. Saved coding tasks shows **Cancel request** for `GENERATING`, `REPAIRING` and `RUNNING` request records, alongside existing check cancellation. A cancelled repair preserves the first failed attempt and launches no further attempt. A proposal finishing just as request cancellation is accepted is retained for inspection, with its verification seal removed and state `CANCELLED`, so it cannot be applied as a successful result. The per-process `_ACTIVE` gate is released in `finally`.
- `_safe_failure_output` now removes the entire Bearer credential from model feedback; its earlier expression removed only the word “Bearer”, leaving the following credential visible. A focused regression covers that case. Saved source/model artifacts remain private local files; this does not establish the Phase 6 guarantee that arbitrary user-authored sources or test output can never contain secrets.

Trace: active frontend/main.jsx renders root App.jsx; App imports components/useAppStore.js, and both desktop/mobile chat use that shared message store. Current setChatMessages is synchronous; persist partialize saves only osintVaultItems, not chat. Mobile also resolves backendUrl/store URL through a mobile/HTTPS fallback; a single saver must use the actual selected target consistently. No store, App, main or chat component was changed in this storage stage. Do not attach two savers or send an old context to a newly selected owner/backend. New frontend/src/utils/conversationOutbox.js::openConversationOutbox opens IndexedDB aibs-conversation-outbox-v1 only on explicit call. heads/entries stores retain backend+owner+conversation-scoped state, complete immutable local source revisions and SHA256. Public queueSnapshot/loadConversation/listConversations/prepareNext/acknowledge/markConflict/closeConversation/close. Input requires stable32hex conversation/request/message IDs, finite plain JSON and unique message IDs, nonnegative safe revisions, valid UTF-8 and title<=2000 Unicode codepoints. Sparse/decorated arrays, symbol-bearing fields and over16MiB reject without silent loss. No tokens, HTTP, app mutation, worker or import-time storage init. Owner is client scope metadata, NOT proof of authentication: future integration must get it from the backend identity endpoint. Same-origin/profile access can inspect local data; no encryption/XSS immunity claim.

queueSnapshot commits source and head compare-and-swap in one strict-durability IDB transaction, so competing tabs cannot both advance from the same local head. Exact request retry deduplicates; changed reuse rejects. prepareNext freezes one request/body/base server revision; newer edits remain distinct. Acknowledge checks identifiers/count/revision/storage/durable/hash form, source stability and order; duplicates do not advance twice. Local source SHA differs from Python canonical server SHA; do not claim independent server-byte verification from its hash shape. Conflict retains everything and stops automatic advancement. Closing marks a view-session record, retains all source/pending entries and permits their later submission; it is NOT a confirmed server/archive save. No pruning/delete path. Missing/blocked browser storage fails without memory fallback,5s open/10s transaction bounds. Transaction completion/strict option and browser-process restart are NOT hardware/power-loss/eviction guarantees. No actual disk-exhaustion case tested.

## v5.329.33: chat-bound automatic memory, controlled verification only

Authoritative continuation October24,2026, source5.329.33/plan1.41. Previous goal turn was progress (v32 IDB prerequisite). This turn actually binds it to desktop/mobile source. Full goal active/incomplete;3 of6 historical limited-local phases, VM explicitly DEFERRED/no setup retries or expanded host fallback. No agents; originals/docs/model versions retained; UI redesign remains later. No production store migration or service start/publication.

New frontend/src/utils/conversationMemory.js::conversationMemoryFor is a WeakMap manager per actual Zustand store. New frontend/src/hooks/useConversationMemory.jsx::useConversationMemory/ConversationMemoryStatus connects effective backend and currentUser UID to protected identity and revision APIs. Both ChatTab.jsx and MobileStehouwerChat.jsx (all8 mirrors) use bound messages/draft setters, masked view while identity changes, disabled composer/send/clear until ready, actual status/history controls and beforeClear. Desktop static Auto-Save Active claim removed. Store/App/main files unchanged; no extra saver instance under React strict/duplicate mounts. Actual normal App imports the root components/store; effective mobile fallback stays supplied by its existing component, with isCurrent replacing the old store-URL equality in archive guards.

Protocol wire format1: first aibs_state_v1 record holds text draft; later aibs_message_v1 records hold complete original JSON dictionaries under message with separate stable32hex envelope IDs. Original message IDs/JSON metadata are retained; undefined object properties follow ordinary JSON omission and unsupported/nonfinite data rejects. No unsent binary-attachment snapshot or external media-byte copying. Old source archives/manuscripts/model files unchanged. Private history is not automatically embedded/trained/global-search inserted; future RAG must decode envelopes and enforce owner/secret review.

Identity is checked before reading/displaying/uploading history; client labels alone grant no authority. Context switches hide old text and abort prior generation. Late bound setters retain updates in their original local context, never the new view. IndexedDB remains readable to same-origin/profile code, not encrypted/XSS-proof. Local capture debounce500ms/max3s during streaming, first immediate; remote750ms. Pending status and beforeunload warning cover dirty current/detached sources; sudden crash/eviction can lose the latest uncommitted interval. This is not zero-loss hardware proof. localTail serializes captures. beforeClear awaits actual pending storage and a closed-view marker before the old utility's unchanged-content/current-context comparison empties the view. New edits/context changes keep chat; closed reload stays empty, while archived picker recovery creates a separate preserved copy.

Remote pump uses one selected backend, frozen request ID/body and full authenticated readback messages/title/receipt comparison before local acknowledgement. No origin fallback, tool/model execution or silent409 merge. Retry preserves request; Save separate copy is explicit. History recovery uses owner/backend-scoped local records or confirmed remote revision reads; no dispatch of restored code. A single unclosed local source can restore without sessionStorage. Unknown preexisting sources use unassigned:profile:<uid> local quarantine, never automatic remote ownership; explicit adoption retains the quarantine source, and denial/retry or a new store can rediscover it. Multiple unassigned sources without a unique pointer require individual review; do not guess. Unsent text drafts restore, but component-local unsent attachments remain separate.

## v5.329.43: preparation controls, scoped history and saved checkpoint

CURRENT: source43/plan1.53, October24. This supersedes the previous next-step UI-wiring instruction. Full goal remains active/incomplete: 3of6 historical limited trusted-local phases, no additional whole-phase completion. VM remains explicitly deferred; actual browser permission/sign-in acceptance remains pending. No browser workaround or Firebase/native publication occurred.

IMPLEMENTED: frontend/src/utils/projectPreparation.js exports buildPreparationPlan, createPreparationClient and preparationClientFor. Explicit selected editable/verification/dependency paths and fixed Python/Node/npm check profiles produce a preparation plan only. Lazy open obtains server identity/history; no network on panel mount. Preview binds exact plan, file roles/hashes and checks. Form edits invalidate it; save uses the frozen preview/request ID. Failed/lost saves retain the same ID/payload for inspect/retry; keeping a pending ID allows a separate request without discarding that evidence. Backend/actor changes abort and hide stale results, and private owned contexts prevent stale follow-up writes. This uses the existing authenticated fetch interceptor, no new client keys or owner authority. Form/unconfirmed-ID cache is in memory only; permanent unsent-draft recovery is not claimed. Retained server preparations are available through history after reload.

UI: CodingPreparationPanel.jsx (PreparationView/default wrapper) is byte-identical in frontend/src/components, frontend/components and each nested components mirror. ChatTab.jsx and MobileStehouwerChat.jsx each add only one import and one panel mount after ConversationMemoryStatus in all4 mirrors. The collapsed panel has objective/files/checks, explicit use-current-draft, preview/save, inspect/recover, pending-ID and paged-history controls. Current chat draft is copied only on the explicit action with matching verified owner/current conversation. Existing conversation/outbox/archive and generation/action handlers are preserved. PREPARED/Tests not run and metadata-only history/Inputs not rechecked are literal UI states; there is no new Run/Apply action.

BACKEND: core/coding_preparation.py::list_preparations(owner,limit=20,cursor=None) scans only its owner-hashed existing journal, holds directories, pages up to50 metadata records with a cooperative0.5sec budget and no payload-byte readback. This is not a hard OS I/O deadline. Missing history does not initialize a store; corrupt/budget-failed history is503, never empty success. Existing preview/prepare/read/recover functions remain AST-identical.
